# Ontology index

Generated by `experiment/playbook-completion/ontology/tools/ontology_check.py` from the module files in
this directory. Do not edit. Product pin `3eaa79cf`. Evidence without a `C-` prefix resolves against the
original submission (`experiment/playbook-uplift/`); `C-` references resolve against
`experiment/playbook-completion/evidence/runs/`.

## How to read the evidence

- **proved**: a Verus proof over the L1/L2 models in `proofs/` (decisive run recorded in `evidence/runs.json`). Proofs are about models; where the code at the pin differs from the proved design, the statement also cites a finding.
- **mc**: an explicit-state model check in `model/`, exhaustive only within the bounds the machine declares (see `MODELS.md`). A model is an executable twin, not the code.
- **trace**: a real execution trace replayed through a machine (completion runs only).
- **live**: observed on IRIS Community 2026.1 in the dedicated container (logs under `$RUN_DIR`).
- **run**: an upstream test set executed here. **ran**: a cited upstream test (`tested:`) that passed in one of those runs. **tested**: an upstream test that asserts the statement; not necessarily run here.
- **inspected**: read in production code at the pin. **assumed**: a named premise in `TRUST.md` or `trust/`. **open**: a decision for the human. **finding**: the code disagrees.

## Gate

**PASS**: 3877 statements in 19 modules, 904 cells, 343 glossary terms (860 local restatements). ## Premises (198)

Statements that rest on a named premise (see the original TRUST.md).

| Id | Statement | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| SYS-P1 | When a process stops, IRIS undoes the writes of its open transaction. | assumed:P-0001 |
| SYS-P2 | Other processes can read a new value before the writer's transaction ends. | assumed:P-0002 |
| SYS-P3 | A lock freed inside a transaction stays held until that transaction ends. | assumed:P-0003 |
| SYS-P4 | When a process stops, IRIS frees every lock it held. | assumed:P-0004 |
| INS-P2.17 | IRIS treats schema names without case, but class package names with case. | assumed:P-0103 |
| INS-P2.18 | IRIS puts a schema's tables and functions in the class package. That package is the schema name with dots for underscores. | assumed:P-0104 |
| INS-P4.5 | IRIS Community 2026.1 has embedded Python but lacks the AI classes. So setup skips the three MCP classes there. | assumed:P-0101; tested:tests/e2e/test_470_setup_compiles_every_class_e2e.py::test_setup_compiles_every_shipped_class_but_the_ai_ones |
| INS-P4.15 | When one class in a list fails to compile, IRIS still compiles each other class it can. | assumed:P-0102 |
| INS-P4.16 | The upstream test session compiles every class one at a time, with no skip rules. On IRIS Community it fails, so live suites there need another deploy. | assumed:P-0106; inspected:iris_vector_graph/_engine/class_deploy.py:78-106 |
| INS-P5.9 | When IRIS makes an SQL routine again, it compiles its class again. It also drops the saved queries that call it. | assumed:P-0107 |
| INS-P6.15 | A grant listing shows a grant made on a whole schema once per routine, with no way named. | assumed:P-0109 |
| INS-P6.16 | A revoke that names no grantor takes away only the current user's own grants. It gives no error. | assumed:P-0110 |
| INS-P7.10 | IRIS keeps the text of each SQL function and view in its catalog, so it can be compared. | assumed:P-0108 |
| INS-P8.10 | Deleting a class that owns a table deletes the table's rows with it. | assumed:P-0111 |
| INS-P10.12 | IRIS checks the caller's SQL rights for dynamic SQL. For a global it checks only the right on the database. | assumed:P-0113 |
| INS-P12 | A user's lack of a right shows in IRIS's error text, which names the right. | assumed:P-0105; tested:tests/unit/test_262_setup_privilege.py::test_missing_privilege_reads_the_privilege_from_iris_text |
| INS-P13 | An SQL name given with no schema is looked up in the process's default schema. | assumed:P-0112 |

| INT-D1 | A FHIR repository is where an InterSystems FHIR server keeps its health data. It sits in the same namespace as IVG. | inspected:iris_src/src/Graph/KG/FHIRGraph.cls:167-226 |

| INT-D3 | A FHIR graph is a named graph that mirrors one FHIR repository. It has one node for each FHIR resource that is not deleted. | inspected:iris_src/src/Graph/KG/FHIRGraph.cls:1-11; inspected:iris_src/src/Graph/KG/FHIRGraph.cls:228-229 |

| INT-D5 | A compartment edge is an edge from a FHIR resource to the patient whose chart holds it. | inspected:iris_src/src/Graph/KG/FHIRGraph.cls:2192-2214 |
| INT-D6 | A FHIR sync is one run that brings a FHIR graph up to date with its FHIR repository. | inspected:iris_src/src/Graph/KG/FHIRGraph.cls:543-628 |

| INT-D9 | A param denylist names the search params whose links a FHIR graph leaves out. | inspected:iris_src/src/Graph/KG/FHIRGraph.cls:1786-1787 |
